# Urban Reign (street-reign) — WIRING INVENTORY

Every extracted file under `teardown/urban-reign/` on main (3,166 files, enumerated from the git tree 2026-10-21), each ending **WIRED** with its consuming path, **FLAGGED** with a named reason, or **REFERENCE** (docs/tooling/provenance). Wiring lane: UR sweep lane 1, 2026-10-21. Companion: `CANON-CONVERSION.md`; decode evidence: PR #429 + `docs/urban-reign/URBAN-REIGN-GATES.md`.

## Summary

| Category | Files | Status |
|---|---|---|
| Character PPKs (`ppks/chars/`) | 72 | WIRED — decoded to skinned GLBs; live in the build (see per-file lines) |
| PR #429 delivered models + tools (section below) | 76+ | WIRED — source artifacts for the game copies |
| Stage PPKs (`ppks/stages/`) | 7 | FLAGGED — stage geometry not yet decoded |
| Mission PPKs + definitions (`missions/`) | 356 | FLAGGED — mission data not decoded |
| Motion packs (`animations/motion-packs/`) | 46 | FLAGGED — indexed; bank conversion pending |
| Motion banks (`motion/`) | 6 | FLAGGED — sampler cracked; bank conversion pending |
| ADX audio (`sound/ok_adx/`) | 1133 | FLAGGED — not converted to game audio assets |
| Sound banks (`audio/`) | 1034 | FLAGGED — not converted to game audio assets |
| VGS voice (`vgs/`) | 88 | FLAGGED — decode documented (VGS-FORMAT.md); not consumed by the game |
| Cutscenes (`cutscenes/`) | 35 | FLAGGED — cutscene data not converted |
| UI assets (`ui/`) | 76 | FLAGGED — documented (UI-SYSTEM.md); game uses its own UI |
| Per-char OBJ dumps (`models/`) | 289 | REFERENCE — bone-local extractions superseded by the PR #429 skinned GLBs |
| Tools (`tools/`) | 11 | REFERENCE — extraction/analysis tooling |
| Docs + maps + listings | 13 | REFERENCE |

## Character PPKs — WIRED (72)

Each `ppks/chars/charNNN.ppk` is the source data for the skinned GLB at `teardown/urban-reign/models/charNNN/charNNN.glb` (PR #429, gates G1–G5 pass), copied into the playable build at `game/assets/models/street-reign/charNNN.glb` and registered as `MODEL_URLS['ur-charNNN']` (`game/js/assets.js`) + roster entry `ur-charNNN` (`game/js/roster-full.js`, bonus tier).

- `teardown/urban-reign/ppks/chars/char001.ppk` → WIRED: `game/assets/models/street-reign/char001.glb` (roster id `ur-char001`)
